## Document 010: 10月美联储FOMC会议纪要：为10月未加息“找一堆理由”，又强烈指引12月“至少加息25基点”

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月美联储FOMC会议纪要：为10月未加息“找一堆理由”，又强烈指引12月“至少加息25基点”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

" 主啊，让我转鹰吧，但再等等 " （ Lord, make me hawkish, but not yet ）。
摘要：
美联储 10 ⽉ FOMC 会议纪要释放⽭盾信号：⼀边为上次会议的暂停加息提供了五花⼋⻔的理
由，⼀边⼜以罕⻅的⼒度向市场预告， 12 ⽉会议⼤概率将⾄少加息 25 个基点。
11 ⽉ 18 ⽇，据追⻛交易台消息，摩根⼤通将这份纪要形容为 " 主啊，让我转鹰吧，但再等
等 " （ Lord, make me hawkish, but not yet ）。
纪要在前瞻指引上措辞清晰、毫不回避。 " ⼏乎所有与会者 " 认为，在基线预期情景下，未来数⽉
进⼀步收紧政策将是适当之举。其中 " 少数⼏位 " 与会者表⽰， 12 ⽉加息幅度或需超过 25 基点——
摩根⼤通分析师 Michael Feroli 判断，这其中可能包括理事 Waller 及另⼀名委员；还有 " 少数⼏
位 " 认为政策或需⾛向 " 更具限制性 " 的⽴场，暗⽰可能还需要额外超过两次 25 基点的加息。

纪要显⽰，尽管前瞻⽴场明显偏鹰，联储官员为 10 ⽉暂停加息所援引的理由却相当多元。综合
纪要措辞与摩根⼤通解读，市场⽬前对 12 ⽉加息 25 基点的预期已获纪要明确背书。
为何 10 ⽉按兵不动：官员给出多重理由
本周三公布的美联储 10 ⽉ 27 ⽇⾄ 28 ⽇货币政策会议纪要显⽰，多数（ most ）与会者⽀持 10 ⽉维
持利率不变。其中许多（ many ）与会者认为，积累更多信息有助于判断适当的货币政策⽴场，
确保通胀及时回落⾄美联储 2% 的⽬标。尽管纪要后⽂中鹰派委员质疑：在随后六周内，市场究
竟能获取多少实质性新信息。
" 少数⼏位 " 委员认为，放慢加息节奏可以给企业和家庭提供更多适应时间； " 数位 " 委员则提出，
真正重要的是联邦基⾦利率的整体路径，⽽⾮单次会议的具体⾏动。相⽐之下，主张 10 ⽉应当
加息的委员理由则更为直接：通胀仍然过⾼，且当前政策限制性尚不充分。
12 ⽉加息路径：纪要指引罕⻅清晰
尽管 10 ⽉暂停加息的理由繁多，纪要对 12 ⽉的前瞻指引却异常明确。 " ⼏乎所有参与者 " 在基线

情景下⽀持进⼀步收紧，这⼀表述已构成较强的政策预信号。
Feroli 在报告中指出，⽆论 10 ⽉暂停的理由为何，该次会议纪要明确指向 12 ⽉加息 25 基点。部分
鹰派委员甚⾄对暂停后能否获得⾜够新信息表⽰怀疑，进⼀步强化了 12 ⽉⾏动的必要性。
经济与⾦融稳定⻛险： AI 回报不及预期被点名
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

纪要在经济前景及委员会讨论层⾯总体符合预期。值得注意的是，在⾦融稳定⻛险讨论环
节， " ⼏乎所有与会者 " 特别提及了⼀项潜在⻛险情景 —— 若 AI 投资回报令投资者失望，可能引发
市场动荡。
但奇怪的是，纪要中没有记录对 2027 年 FOMC 会议⽇程变更的任何讨论。
此前，美联储主席沃什曾宣布，美联储将从 2027 年起调整会议制度：每年⼋次会议中，将有⼀
次专⻔⽤于宏观货币政策战略讨论，不进⾏利率表决； 2028 年此类战略会议增⾄两次。

~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
以上精彩内容来⾃追⻛交易台。
更详细的解读，包括实时解读、⼀线研究等内容，请加⼊【追⻛交易台 ▪ 年度会员】
“ 在讨论⾦融稳定问题时，⼏乎所有（ almost all ）与会者都谈到了相关⻛险：如果 AI 投资的回报
令投资者感到失望，可能会引发⼀系列问题。
许多（ Many ）与会者探讨了若发⽣ AI 相关的资产价格重估，可能会带来哪些后果。
多数（ most ）与会者指出， AI 相关的债务增⻓迅速，且其中很⼤⼀部分是通过复杂且不透明的
融资结构形成的。
少数（ a few ）与会者提到， AI 相关的⻛险敞⼝正⽇益集中在少数⼏家企业⾝上。 ”

### Page 4

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 043: 20261015【投研報告】市場快訊_通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261015【投研報告】市場快訊_通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (市場評論)
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決

不另行通知。
通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期，Fed 再升息風險重新升高：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

### Page 2

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表 AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與 AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展，引發市場對未來 DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮，記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及 AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與 AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵，仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與 AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

不另行通知。
3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺，留意 10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者，受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與 AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

### Page 4

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前 AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

市場焦點
美國經濟
• 第三季GDP初值季增年率為3.4%，高於市場估計的3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。美國9月個人消
費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，年增3.6%；核心PCE指數月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局、市場對
於AI投資前景的疑慮持續，可能加大短期股市波動風險。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存，
持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
期中選舉
• 美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次，
將提高川普後續推動政策的難度。觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期，期中選舉的隔年
通常是股市表現最佳的年度，其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後，政治不確定性降低，市場對

政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
中國
• 9月工業企業利潤年成長14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快，中國工業利潤改善，主要由AI
硬體與能源價格上漲帶動，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企業則是能
源價格上漲的最大受益者之一，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線，但內需疲
弱與價格競爭問題未解，投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。

美國第三季GDP成長超預期、通膨壓力續增
• 美國第三季經濟成長率(GDP)創一年來最大增幅：美國經濟分析局(BEA)公布，第三季GDP初值季增年率為3.4%，高於市場
估計的3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。企業AI投資火熱、消費者開支保持韌性，抵銷了油價上漲衝擊。
• 9月數據顯示通膨壓力續增，但符合預期：美國9月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，寫3月以來最大月增幅；年增幅
3.6%。剔除食物與能源的9月核心PCE指數，月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 市場反應：由於新一輪通膨數據符合預期，市場未改變聯準會12月利率決議升息一碼的預測。然而，有跡象顯示實質個人
消費支出開始轉弱，儲蓄率也有所下降，市場開始押注通膨可能已經見頂。從聯邦利率期貨市場數據來看，市場對於明年升

息空間的預期已較上個月收斂(九月底預測升息2~3碼→目前預測1~2碼)，
• 觀點：美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局、市場對於AI投資前景的疑慮持續，
可能加大短期股市波動風險。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存，持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
資料來源：Bloomberg，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.30。

聯準會貨幣決議與聲明稿差異
資料來源：Federal Reserve（10/28、9/16 聲明）；鉅亨網，2026/10/29。以上為聲明重點整理。
2 / 5
10月決議｜按兵不動
3.75% – 4.00%
10票贊成、2票反對
經濟措辭轉強 「穩健擴張」改為「強勁擴張」；國內支出具韌性， 生產力與資本投資表現強。
通膨仍未解除 聲明維持「通膨居高」的判斷，未再宣稱本次升息有 助加快回到2%目標。
政策訊號更具彈性 改稱需要更多資訊判斷適當政策立場，未承諾下一步 升息或降息。
關鍵變化
理事庫克(Lisa D. Cook) 和克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪克(Beth M. Hammack)主張本次升息1碼，
形成2張鷹派反對票；9月決議則為一致通過。利率不變不等於轉向寬鬆，委員會內部對通膨風險仍

有不同判斷。
市場解讀：本次是「暫停觀察」；12月是否再升息，仍取決於通膨、需求與能源價格。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
資料來源：Federal Reserve 10/28會後記者會；Reuters、Investing.com，2026/10/28。
3 / 5
01｜市場利率反映緊縮壓力
華許指出，9月與10月兩次會議之
間，美國公債名目與實質殖利率明
顯走高；市場利率可先行反映成長
、通膨與風險變化，不必等政策利
率調整。
政策含意
長天期殖利率仍受通膨預期、期限
溢酬及公債供需影響；政策利率不
變，不代表債市壓力解除。
02｜AI投資與生產力
華許認為美國經濟尚未過熱，AI投
資推升需求，但生產力也有改善；
聯準會將持續評估新科技對產出、
就業與物價的中長期影響。
政策含意
AI支出可支撐企業成長，也伴隨資本
支出回收與融資風險；應檢視現金流
、投資報酬及生產力是否實際提升。

03｜政策制度與工具再檢視
聯準會規劃2027年安排1次、2028
年安排2次專注貨幣政策策略的會
議，不進行利率表決或發布政策聲
明；另將檢視資產負債表政策。
政策含意
制度調整不等於政策轉向寬鬆；仍須
觀察準備金、資產負債表操作及經濟
數據，評估金融條件如何變化。
解讀：華許除談利率外，也聚焦市場利率、AI生產力及政策制度調整；投資判斷仍應回到數據、企業獲利與金融條件。

聯準會利率決議摘要暨投資策略建議
資料來源：Federal Reserve；Reuters、InvestingLive；鉅亨網，2026/10/29。市場價格可能隨後續交易更新。
4 / 5
政策決議 利率維持 3.75% – 4.00% 經濟強、通膨高；政策下一步保留彈性
美股
短線受利率預期與科技股評價牽動
；AI投資支撐獲利，但仍需檢視現
金流與資本支出回報。
美債
若升息預期升溫，短天期殖利率較
敏感；長天期亦受通膨預期、發債
供給與期限溢酬影響。
美元／黃金
美元可能隨利差預期波動；黃金同
時受實質利率、美元及地緣政治避
險需求影響。
投資策略建議｜情境式配置
股票 保留核心持股，避免追價；優先關注獲利能見度、現金流與定價能力較佳企業。
債券 以分散到期日與信用風險為主；可分批配置高品質債券，避免單押長天期利率下行。

多元資產 保留流動性與再平衡空間；若通膨再升、殖利率上行，分批進場並控管整體波動。
後續觀察：美國通膨與就業數據、能源價格、企業財報，以及市場對12月政策的重新定價。

• 美國期中選舉登場，國會席次變化牽動川普施政：美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，為川普第二任期上任以來首場全國性選
舉，除國會議員改選外，亦包括州長及其他公職人員選舉。若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次，將提
高川普後續推動政策的難度。不過，川普仍可透過發布行政命令等方式，在一定程度上繞過國會的立法制衡，持續推動其政

策議程。
• 本次選舉結果可能耗費一週才能明朗：回顧2006至2022年近五屆美國期中選舉，其中四屆的參眾兩院控制權於投票當晚或
隔日即大致明朗，僅2022年因關鍵席次競爭激烈、郵寄選票計票耗時較長，直至投票後第8天才確認兩院席次歸屬。考量本
次參議院選情膠著，加上川普執政以來美國政治極化持續加劇，不排除本次選舉結果需耗費一週才能明朗，期間市場也可能
面臨額外的不確定性。

期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度
• 期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度：觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期，期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳
的年度。根據1940至2025年統計，標普500指數第三年平均報酬率達16.5%，高於第一年的8.3%、第二年的4.2%及第四年
的6.9%；中位數報酬率更達18.9%。其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後，政治不確定性降低，市場對政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，
且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
• 政黨與國會組成影響有限，基本面仍是關鍵： 進一步觀察政黨差異，1940年以來，第三年由共和黨總統執政時，標普500指
數平均報酬率約18.2%，高於民主黨總統執政時的14.9%，但兩者均呈現正報酬，顯示總統所屬政黨並非決定股市表現的唯

一因素。國會組成亦可能影響政策推動與市場預期，例如共和黨總統搭配民主黨國會時，第三年平均報酬率約15.6%；若國
會呈現混合執政，平均報酬率則達23.1%。

能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升
• 能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升：9月工業企業利潤年成長14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快。但前三季度累計增速
從1到8月的15.7%小幅放緩至15.5%，利潤改善主要反映了能源和AI硬體價格的上漲，其中AI硬體價格仍是高技術製造業利潤
的重要支撐。前三季度，高技術製造業利潤年成長52.0%，與1到8月54.7%的增速大體相當。拉動整體利潤成長約10個百分點，
貢獻率約三分之二。值得注意的是，利潤成長仍主要集中於少數相關行業，其他企業部門整體依然處境艱難，內需疲弱疊加價
格競爭激烈令利潤受到擠壓。
• 投資建議：中國工業利潤改善，主要由AI硬體與能源價格上漲帶動，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企

業則是能源價格上漲的最大受益者之一，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線，但內需疲弱與價格競爭問題未解，
投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.27
能源、AI價格飆升提振中國相關行業利潤增速

中國科技公司享受負殖利率差
• 中國科技公司享受負殖利率差：中國在經濟成長放緩與寬鬆貨幣
政策下，借貸成本持續下降，10年期公債殖利率由年初1.86%降
至1.63%，使企業融資條件更加寬鬆，相較之下，美國受經濟韌
性與高利率政策影響，10年期公債殖利率升至約5%，企業整體融
資成本明顯提高。此外2025年中國大型科技企業在境內債券市場
融資規模有限，僅為約74億美元，而且支付的殖利率，平均比同
風 險 層 級 的 債 券 高 出18個 基 點，但現在溢 價 已 經 變 為 折 價 ，
2026年迄今，中國科技企業已在境內發債約100億美元，新發科
技債殖利率平均折價6個基點，顯示機構投資者信心發生了顯著轉
變，大幅降低了行業推進AI建設的資金成本，會有上述現象是由
於中國經濟通縮，難以承受更高利率，因此官方須維持較寬鬆的

金融條件，科技企業剛剛開始通過債券市場融資，預料債券將受
到投資者關注。
• 展望後市：低成本債券融資對中國科技行業構成利好，與IPO不
同，債券市場為企業提供了一個可重複利用的融資渠道，在股市
劇烈波動之際，對投資人是另一種資產配置，可優先關注具資金
優勢與應用場景的大型科技龍頭，以及受惠於AI算力需求成長的
伺服器、網通設備和資料中心產業。
中國科技公司享受負殖利率差
中美國債走勢分化

AI產業鏈融資警訊：資金缺口正在擴散
• 資本支出與現金流的嚴重失衡：AI生態系中的巨額投資正考驗著企業，一旦企業營運現金流不足以支持基礎設施建設，就必
須高度依賴外部融資(發債或增資)，使其更容易受到資本市場波動與利率變化的衝擊。AI算力提供商資金缺口最為顯著，其
資本支出已接近營運現金流3倍(284%)，需要投入大額資金採購高端GPU並對外出租算力，數據中心資本支出佔現金流的比
例已從2022年11月(ChatGPT問世)1倍(104%)快速爬升至1.3倍(128%)，超大規模雲服務商資本支出占現金流比例已從2022
年底40%翻倍飆升至73%。在彭博研究統計的8家超大型雲業者中，出現自由現金流為負(資金缺口)公司數量已從2022年的
1家增加至4家(達50%)。
• 產業鏈錯綜複雜，風險具連鎖效應：從上游晶片製造商、硬體開發商、建築商、電力/公用事業，到中游的數據中心與雲端

服務商，再到下游的軟體與數據管理公司，其銷售與支付合約彼此高度緊密相連。當算力提供商或數據中心出現資金吃緊時，
風險會迅速向上游傳遞，導致項目延期、融資成本攀升，甚至延後整個生態系的收入實現時程。目前除了雲端與數據中心外，
硬體開發商與建築商中出現資金缺口的企業比例也在上升。
資金缺口正蔓延至人工智能產業鏈一半的層級
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
算力提供商和數據中心的資本支出超過現金流

市場定價轉變：借貸成本上升與股票估值修正
• 資本市場已經開始針對資金缺口進行風險定價：在資金缺口最大的AI算力提供商、數據中心以及超大規模雲服務商中，其發
行的公司債相較於美國國債的殖利率差(利差)顯著拓寬，高於一般投資級債券或高收益債券的表現，顯示債券市場對其違約
與融資風險要求更高的風險補償。由於大量現金流被基礎設施建設掏空，且AI應用端的獲利回收速度未達市場預期，投資人
不再願意給予極高溢價，整體AI產業鏈的預期本益比出現明顯修正。
• 小結與投資建議：當前AI產業正在經歷基礎設施過渡期，資本密集的算力出租、數據中心建設正遭受強烈的資金擠壓，若未
來1~2年內下游應用端的商業化營收無法順利銜接，依賴高度槓桿與外部融資的企業將面臨嚴峻的流動性挑戰。對AI算力出
租商及部分高負債的數據中心開發商保持謹慎態度，一旦信貸市場收緊或借貸成本升高，利潤與營運將受重創。晶片製造商

的經營現金流持續成長，財務結構改善，選擇擁有強大訂價權與現金流護城河的上游半導體巨頭，安全邊際最高，電力與公
用事業資本支出佔現金流比例相對穩定且受惠於剛性需求。超大規模雲服務商中表現差異大，優先選擇資產負債表健全、資
產回報率高且擁有傳統核心業務能持續提供穩定現金流的科技巨頭，遠離需大量靠發債維持資本支出的個股。
超大規模雲服務商和數據中心的利差擴大
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
隨著借貸成本波動加劇，AI股權估值走弱

顧 • 利率可能維持高檔更久，但收益價值正在浮現：近月以
來美國10年期公債殖利率一度升抵5.36%，30年期接
近5.7%，反映市場重新評價未來利率中樞、財政赤字
及資金需求等因素。雖然長天期債券價格仍可能受到利
率波動影響，但高殖利率已大幅提高新進場資金的收益
起點，投資焦點應由預測利率轉向評估收益價值。
• 債券標售與資金流顯示配置需求逐步回溫：10月美國
財政部10年期、20年期及30年期公債標售需求維持穩
健，投標倍數約介於2.5至2.8倍區間；同時，美國債券
基金已連續26週呈現資金淨流入，顯示市場未必認為
利率已見頂，但已開始接受5%以上殖利率的長期配置
價值。
• 收益優先取代押注利率轉折：在Higher for Longer環
境下，債券的核心功能重新回到穩定收益、分散風險與

降低組合波動。建議可以3至7年期美國公債及高評等
投資級公司債作為核心收益部位，長天期債券則採分批
布局方式，兼顧收益來源及未來利率循環變化帶來的潛
在機會。

4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣(DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins from ASP Pass-Through and Richer Mix
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), 
beating Citi/cons by 27%/45% on stronger-than-expected margins and 
non-op gains. GPM of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8 ppts YoY) topped Citi/cons 
at 15.8%/14.5%, as ASP hikes passed through rising component costs while 
moving-avg inventory costs lagged, alongside a richer high-end product 
mix. For 4Q26E, we expect further ASP increases and a rising server 
contribution to largely offset seasonal shipment declines. Into 2027, we 
expect PC demand to stay weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases moderately. We raise 2026E-28E earnings and our 
TP to NT$145 (10x 2027E EPS). We stay Neutral as the share price already 
reflects the pass-through upside.
3Q26: Margin-led beat on cost pass-through and better mix — MSI reported 
3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), beating Citi/cons forecasts 
by 27%/45%, mainly on stronger-than-expected margins and non-op gains. GPM 
of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8ppts YoY) came in above Citi/cons at 15.8%/14.5%. 
Mgmt. attributed the upside to 1) ASP increase, as MSI raised prices in line with 
suppliers’ cost increases while its moving-average inventory costs rose less; and 2) 
a higher mix of high-end products. Non-op gains, including FX gains of over 
NT$100mn, further boosted the bottom line.
Price hikes to continue; 2027 PC market to keep shrinking — We reiterate our view 
that 4Q26 shipments will decline seasonally from the 3Q26 peak. However, mgmt. 
expects rising raw material costs and continued pass-through to keep lifting ASPs, 
offsetting lower volumes and making the seasonal decline milder than in prior years. 
The server business should also contribute more, potentially reaching a high-single-
digit revenue share. Into 2027, we expect weak PC demand amid elevated costs, 
with MSI’s MB and PC shipments declining by over 10%. Pass-through room should 
narrow as memory price increases moderately, though margins should stay elevated 
for a while, as mgmt. expects memory supply to stay tight in the coming quarters.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 6-27%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through and higher margin assumptions. 
We adjust TP to NT$145 (from NT$140), implying 10x 2027E EPS (from 12x). We 
believe the share price already reflects MSI’s opportunity to pass component costs 
through to end products, and we expect limited upside going forward, given that the 
PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain. Maintain Neutral.

Bull/Bear: Micro-Star (2377.TW)

### Page 6

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
13 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$145 is based on 10x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.
If you are visually impaired and would like to speak to a Citi representative regarding the details

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

M
Update
Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Component supply to improve: While component supply could stay tight near
term, Delta expects to secure bigger volume in the coming quarters, so it should
have better order fulfillment ahead. Oct-26 monthly sales seems to point to double-
digit% QoQ revenue growth in 4Q26, in our view, and it is likely to see better than
seasonal growth in 1Q27. It expects the component supply tightness to start easing

in 2Q27.
HVDC contribution minor  near term: It should start to ship HVDC power racks in
4Q26, but the contribution should be relatively small. For the GPU platform,
scheduled for 2H27, Delta sees 80VDC as the only reference design as of now,
although it does not seem to be finalized yet. Our conversations indicate most
investors do not have that as the base case, so there could be upside to consensus
estimates in 2028, if that ends up being the case.

Fuel cell another driver longer term: Delta will have some pilot production in late
2026. Its BoD has approved a greenfield project for the fuel cell business. This fits
well into its power delivery business, especially amid the power constraints that
hyperscalers are being confronted with now.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28e EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 59% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected global economic growth

n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 198: 3324雙鴻 國票

Source PDF: all report pdf/3324雙鴻 國票.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 9

### Page 1

請參閱末頁之聲明

2026.11.19 
徐文華 C00215@ibfs.com.tw 
3324.TT 雙鴻 
 4Q26 新品進入量產

[法說會更新]

公司簡介 
雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，主要生產散熱器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，
應用於筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、伺服器等領域，伺服器主要終端客戶為美系 Tier 1 
CSP，消費性電子客戶包含 Apple (AAPL US)、Dell (DELL US)、HP (HPQ US)、宏碁
(2353)、華碩(2357)等。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,260 
【前次投資建議】買進，1,775 
【大盤指數/股價】40195 / 979

➢ 3Q26AI 新舊品交替、消費性產品疲弱，毛利率低於預期  
雙鴻 3Q26 營收 QoQ+12.5%，YoY+64.3%，毛利率 25.7%，營益率
13.9%，EPS 為 11.59 元，毛利率、獲利低於投研部預期。公司 3Q26 營
收季增、年增，是受惠於 GB300 的加單效應，3Q26 伺服器營收占比已
經達總營收的 81%，消費性產品則是市況疲弱；因消費性產品之產品組
合不佳，且 AI 伺服器處於新舊品轉換期，伺服器有多項新設計進行試
產，導致毛利率 QoQ-2.8ppts，YoY-4.1ppts，連帶使營益率下滑。 
➢ 2027 年伺服器營收目標占比 90% 
雙鴻 10 月營收 33.7 億元 MoM+16.9%，YoY+44.7%，4Q26 有 Vera 
Rubin 與 ASIC 散熱新產品進入量產階段，若放量進度順利，受惠於營
運規模擴大，OPEX%將是下降趨勢，毛利率亦有望回升，營運動能可優
於 3Q26，投研部預估雙鴻 4Q26 營收 114.7 億元 QoQ+17.2%，
YoY+50.95%，毛利率 27.6%，營益率 16.5%，EPS 為 15.36 元。 
目前 AI 伺服器晶片所需的解熱效能約 3000W，根據半導體業者的晶片
設計規劃，2027~2028 年會往 6000W 發展，帶動散熱產品規格升級。
雙面水冷板(Double-Sided Cold Plate) ASP 約為單面產品的 1.5 倍。此
外，下一世代 AI 伺服器會使用 MCL (Micro-channel Lid)，雙鴻已配合
客戶開發中。雙鴻表示，2027 年 ASIC 伺服器成長性很大，公司的伺服
器營收占比可望進一步拉升至 90%，因產品複雜度升級，毛利率也能優
於 2026 年。  
➢ 建議逢低買進，目標價 1,260 元  
雙鴻 3Q26 毛利率低於預期，若 4Q26 Vera Rubin、ASIC 新品放量順
利，投研部認為 4Q26~1H27 公司的營運動能將逐漸轉強。雙鴻歷史 PE
區間為 8X~36X，平均 PE 多位於 15X~20X，近期 AI 伺服器產業利空
頻傳，股價動能集體受到壓抑，考量雙鴻 PE 已接近下緣，美方加嚴 AI
審查也不等於全面停止 AI DC 建置，AI 仍是具有成長性的產業，建議逢
低買進，目標價 1,260 元(2027F PE 15X)。

個股報告

3Q26AI 新舊品交替、消費性產品疲弱，毛利率低於預期 
雙鴻 3Q26 營收 QoQ+12.5%，YoY+64.3%，毛利率 25.7%，營益率 13.9%，EPS 為
11.59 元，毛利率、獲利低於投研部預期。公司 3Q26 營收季增、年增，是受惠於 GB300
的加單效應，3Q26 伺服器營收占比已經達總營收的 81%，消費性產品則是市況疲弱；
因消費性產品之產品組合不佳，且 AI 伺服器處於新舊品轉換期，伺服器有多項新設計
進行試產，導致毛利率 QoQ-2.8ppts，YoY-4.1ppts，連帶使營益率下滑。 
圖 1：雙鴻產品組合 2021~2026F

資料來源：國票投顧 
2027 年伺服器營收目標占比 90% 
雙鴻 10 月營收 33.7 億元 MoM+16.9%，YoY+44.7%，4Q26 有 Vera Rubin 與 ASIC
散熱新產品進入量產階段，若放量進度順利，受惠於營運規模擴大，OPEX%將是下降
趨勢，毛利率亦有望回升，營運動能可優於 3Q26，投研部預估雙鴻 4Q26 營收 114.7
億元 QoQ+17.2%，YoY+50.95%，毛利率 27.6%，營益率 16.5%，EPS 為 15.36 元。 
目前 AI 伺服器晶片所需的解熱效能約 3000W，根據半導體業者的晶片設計規劃，
2027~2028 年會往 6000W 發展，帶動散熱產品規格升級。雙面水冷板(Double-Sided 
Cold Plate)ASP 約為單面產品的 1.5 倍。此外，下一世代 AI 伺服器會使用 MCL (Micro-
channel Lid)，雙鴻已配合客戶開發中。雙鴻表示，2027 年 ASIC 伺服器成長性很大，
公司的伺服器營收占比可望進一步拉升至 90%，因產品複雜度升級，毛利率也能優於
2026 年。 
2027 年泰國擴產進度不變 
近期 AI 伺服器產業充斥利空消息：Tier 1 CSP 自由現金流轉負(投資人質疑 AI 投資的
變現能力)、NVIDIA 執行長黃仁勳下台恐影響未來 GPU 開發進度、美國民主黨可能
加嚴對 AI DC 建置/AI 晶片進出口之審查等，市場憂心 AI 發展將放緩。雙鴻認為，各
家 CSP 會持續建置算力，如算力不足，將輸掉 AI 競賽，就目前訂單來看，2027 年 AI
伺服器水冷散熱市場樂觀，公司 2026、2027 年 CAPEX 都預計達 40~50 億元，對泰
國的工廠建設維持原訂計畫。

雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國，泰國廠布局以 AI 伺服器
水冷散熱為重點：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產，主要生產 cold plate、manifold、
Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/月、manifold 3k/月；2)泰
國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產 cold plate、manifold，並預留電源水冷
產能，若各產品線產能順利拉升，未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍；3)泰國三期規劃在
4Q26 動工、2H27 投產，負責生產 cold plate、CDU、Rack；4)泰國四期預計 2028
年動工；5)中國廠區負責生產筆電、高階顯示卡、伺服器散熱相關產品，整體上，以
氣冷模組較多，也有少量的 cold plate、manifold；6)台灣主力生產 CDU (Cooling 
Distribution Unit)，並負責高階與高精度液冷產品的研發和樣品試產。 
建議逢低買進，目標價 1,260 元 
雙鴻 3Q26 毛利率低於預期，若 4Q26 Vera Rubin、ASIC 新品放量順利，投研部認
為 4Q26~1H27 公司的營運動能將逐漸轉強。雙鴻歷史 PE 區間為 8X~36X，平均 PE
多位於 15X~20X，近期 AI 伺服器產業利空頻傳，股價動能集體受到壓抑，考量雙鴻
PE 已接近下緣，美方加嚴 AI 審查也不等於全面停止 AI DC 建置，AI 仍是具有成長性
的產業，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,260 元(2027F PE 15X)。

股價相對大盤走勢

世芯-KY(3661 TT) Buy

新客戶加入，挹注 2027 年營運成長，建議 Buy。  
投資建議：世芯-KY 受惠北美 CSP 客戶 N3 AI 加速器量產，
2026 年營運大幅成長；展望 2027 年，除既有 N3 專案持續放量
外，美系新客戶約 10 億美元專案預計自 2Q27 起貢獻量產營收，
將推動營收及獲利進一步成長。中長期 N2 AI 加速器亦持續推
進，預計 2028 年進入量產，成為下一階段成長動能，預估 2026
年及 2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 113.66 元及 193.02 元。惟隨量產
(COT)業務占比提高，營收成長未必同步帶動毛利率提升，且
CSP 持續提升自主設計能力，將是影響世芯-KY 長期商業模式
及評價的重要風險。我們認為上述風險主要影響中長期估值上
限，但未來 12 個月仍受惠 N3 放量及新客戶加入，基本面成長
趨勢明確。因此建議 Buy，目標價 4,635 元 (PER24X)。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元：北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片已
正式啟動量產出貨，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億
元，QoQ+240.99%。晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營
收產生約 1 億美元之負面影響。受量產業務營收佔比拉升影響，
3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個百
分點。3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
取得美系新客戶訂單: 世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽
訂重大合約並展開執行，該案量約 10 億美元，合約已正式生效
並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季增加，該專案確
定應用於 AI 領域。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對
象，非中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出
規模，研判最具可能性之潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之
北美雲端巨頭；世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切
入合作，有效緩解客戶端工程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指
出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全新商業模式之開端，
該新客戶將躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。

2 群益投顧 
  世芯-KY ESG：  
世芯電子身為全球 IC 設計服務領導廠商，為確保所有產品皆為符合環境安
全衛生規定之綠色產品，對供應鏈管理不遺餘力，與供應商合作將綠色環保
的政策及概念推廣到整個供應，並一起朝向減少地球環境之衝擊共同努力，
其中主要包括： 
有害物質管制 
符合世芯電子相關規範及國內、外法令等國際環境規範 
滿足世芯電子新外包商評鑑程序 
滿足世芯電子外包商管理程序 
滿足世芯電子環境管理物質管理程序 
歐盟危害物質限用指令 (EU RoHS Compliance) 
歐盟新化學品政策 (REACH) 
無鹵材料 (Halogen Free) 
綠色物料之承認管理 
供應商提出第三方對產品生產材料所作的環境物質分析報告或證明文件。 
綠色資訊平台之建置 
建構系統化之 IT 資料庫輔助管理系統，藉此平台，便利上下游廠商綠色零
件之交換，並帶動上游廠商綠色零件供應的能力，降低環保要求的衝擊程
度。 
建立環境管理系統 - 供應商必須擁有 ISO14001 環境管理系統或其他相關
環境管理系統相關驗證。 
無衝突礦產保證書-(Declaration of Metal Conflict-Free) 
衝突金屬係指來自剛果民主共和國衝突礦區之礦物，類別有鈮鉭鐵礦，錫
石，黑鎢礦與黃金等。這些礦物提煉成鉭(Ta)、錫(Sn)、鎢(W) 、鈷(Co) 和
金 (Au) 等，分別用於電子和其他產品。世芯電子強烈要求供應商提供的原
物料中不得間接或直接使用衝突礦產。 
企業社會責任 (CSR) - 確保供應商落實企業社會責任。 
業務連續性計劃 (BCP) 
供應商須有一套基於業務運行規律的流程，使組織在突發事件面前能夠迅
速作出反應，以確保關鍵業務功能可以持續，而不造成業務中斷或業務流程
本質的改變。 
綠色管理稽核 - 世芯-KY 每年持續對供應商進行稽核、改善及教育訓練，
經由系統化運作與管理，確保提供品質精良之卓越產品給客戶與消費者。

### Page 3

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

3 群益投顧 
N3 貢獻增加，3Q26 營收 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%，稅後淨利 32.51 億
元，QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元： 
北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片 已於 05/2026 正式啟動量產出貨，之後營
收逐月向上，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%。
在產能限制方面，晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營收產生約 1 億
美元之負面影響，若無此類供應限制，單季營收原可增加 1 億美元。資料中
心以外之其他應用在 3Q26 合計創造超過 1 億美元營收，其中中國車用 ADAS
需求維持強勁，客戶次世代晶片如期於 4Q26 進行流片(Tape-out)。  
3Q26 各應用佔公司營收比重，HPC 佔公司營收比重 87%，佔比仍為最高，
相較 2Q26 的 50%與 1Q26 的 70%大幅提升。消費性電子(Consumer)佔比降
至 8%，低於 2Q26 的 16%與 1Q26 的 17%。 Networking 佔 5%，較 2Q26 的
32%與 1Q26 的 10%下降。利基型(Niche，含超級電腦、虛擬貨幣、柏青哥
機台、醫療設備與監視系統)佔 0%，低於 2Q26 的 1%與 1Q26 的 3%。  
製程營收比重，N3/N2 佔公司營收比重大幅躍升至 80%，較 2Q26 的 47%與
1Q26 的 21%大幅增加，主因北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器持續放量出貨。
7nm/5nm 佔比降至 15%，較 2Q26 的 40%下滑。16/12nm 佔比為 3%，較 2Q26
的 6%下降。28nm 及以下製程佔比為 1%，低於 2Q26 的 7%。  
地區營收分布隨著 N3 加速器放量出貨，地區分布再度出現結構性轉變：北
美地區佔比穩居第一大市場，達 84%(2Q26 為 51%， 1Q26 為 23%)。亞太地
區佔比降至 12%(2Q26 為 25%， 1Q26 為 47%)，車用晶片出貨維持穩健。其
他地區佔 4%(2Q26 為 17%， 1Q26 為 22%)。日本地區佔 1%(2Q26 為 7%，
1Q26 為 8%)。   
毛利率方面，3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個
百分點，為產品組合的最差情境，主因量產營收比重大幅拉升，致使委託設
計 (NRE)營收貢獻比例低於預期，加上數個專案之 NRE 認列產生遞延所致。
不過營業利益與稅後淨利同步創高。3Q26 世芯-KY 稅後淨利 32.51 億元，
QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
N2 專案將在 2026 年底 Tape-Out：  
世芯-KY 承接之美系 CSP 次世代 N2 AI 加速器專案，將採用異質小晶片
(Heterogeneous Chiplet) 架構。晶片總面積維持在約 4 個光罩尺寸，但相較
於 N3 世代，N2 專案之運算晶粒(Compute Die) 配置、內部互連架構
(Interconnect)及封裝型式(2.5D/3D)均出現顯著改動。由於小晶片設計涉及
多個獨立晶( 包括主運算晶粒與 I/O 晶粒) ，各晶粒之開發週轉時間
(Turnaround Time)不同，專案將採取分階段進行多次定案(Tape-out)之模式。
2026 年底 Tape-out ， 2027 年底放量 
次世代 N2 加速器已進入最終實作階段，預計自 4Q26 開始展開流片，目標
於 4Q27 達成小量試產(Low volume production) ，並於 2028 年迎來量產放
量，推動世芯-KY 自 2028 年初邁入全新成長階段。N2 與 N3 在晶片設計複
雜度上有顯著差異，N3 主要為 SoC 設計結合 2.5D 封裝，而 N2 雖維持 2.5D
封裝架構，但已全面轉向 Chiplet 架構搭配全環繞閘極(GAA)製程技術。世
芯 -KY 負責 N2 晶片各個部分之實體設計並進行整合，其在 N2 專案之設計
參與深度與整體專案規模均顯著大於 N3。專案不依賴特定同業之第三方 IP，

### Page 4

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

4 群益投顧 
均採用通用型解決方案。在商業與生產模式上，世芯-KY 為 N2 之唯一實體
設計服務商，而在量產階段，客戶目前承諾僅會有客戶本身與世芯-KY 兩方
直接向台積電下單投片，世芯-KY 在 N2 之供應鏈地位與競爭優勢將較 N3
更為穩固。關於小晶片整合，包含 I/O Die 在內之晶片均由世芯-KY 統籌實
體設計，未來若因應 UALink 或 NVLink Fusion 等技術規格變更推出其他版
本之 I/O Die，世芯-KY 亦具備承接物理設計之先機。 
COT 合作模式短中期是有利世芯-KY，但長期要觀察 CSP 廠的全面自主設
計的進度： 
各大 CSP 為降低對傳統純 ASIC 廠商(如 Broadcom)高定價的依賴並實現
Cost-down，正全面轉向 COT 模式(客戶負責前端設計，世芯負責實體設計
與後端整合)。專案朝向「運算晶粒(Compute die)與  I/O 晶粒分工設計」發
展，世芯已與潛在客戶洽談負責運算晶粒並與外部 I/O 晶片整合的模式。
CSP 客戶轉向 COT 模式，本質上源於 3 項戰略考量： 
避免供應商鎖定(Vendor Lock-in) ：多數競爭對手採用垂直整合的封閉模式
(Captive Model)，從 IP、設計服務到封裝一條龍自主掌控，客戶一旦導入即
難以切換。CSP 客戶對此高度警惕，傾向採用第三方開放解決方案以保有供
應商多元化的彈性，降低單一供應商造成的議價劣勢與供應鏈風險。 
成本透明度與預算自主性：COT 模式下，客戶對晶圓、IP、設計服務及其他
供應鏈成本具有較高的掌握度，實際報酬及成本分攤方式則依個別專案合
約而定。相較於封閉模式下由供應商主導整體產品定價，COT 模式有助於
客戶提高成本控管能力與預算可預測性。 
規模擴張下的成本節省潛力： AI 晶片專案的出貨量與單位價值均遠高於傳
統晶片，隨著量產規模持續放大，COT 模式下透明且固定的利潤結構所帶
來的成本節省效益將等比例放大，對 CSP 客戶的財務影響不容忽視。 
然而，COT 模式對世芯-KY 的財務表現構成若干限制： 
COT 模式下的獲利空間取決於合約條件與服務範圍：COT 模式下，客戶通
常對專案成本及供應鏈有較高的掌握度，世芯-KY 的獲利則取決於其承接
的設計服務範圍、專案管理責任及個別合約的報酬安排。相較於自行掌握完
整產品定價與供應鏈的模式，若專案採成本加成或事先約定服務報酬的方
式，世芯-KY 透過晶片價格上漲或供應鏈成本變動擴大利潤的空間可能較
為有限。因此，未來毛利率及獲利表現，仍須觀察各專案的服務內容、合約
條件及營收組合變化。 
多方協作帶來的執行複雜度與供應鏈風險： COT 模式涉及晶圓廠、IP 供應
商、基板廠、封裝廠及測試廠等多個外部夥伴的協同整合，任一環節的產能
短缺或交期延誤均可能波及整體專案進度。世芯-KY 在 N7 世代曾因 ABF 基
板短缺而承受供應鏈壓力，此一歷史案例揭示開放生態系模式下供應鏈管
理的內在脆弱性。儘管公司已大幅增加供應商來源以分散風險，但多源管理
本身亦帶來額外的協調成本與品質控管挑戰。 
IP 自主能力缺失，技術話語權受限：世芯-KY 目前選擇與 Synopsys 等第三
方 IP 供應商合作，而非自行開發 IP。此策略雖降低研發資本支出，卻也意
味著公司在核心技術環節缺乏自主掌控能力，在特定 IP 出現供應限制或授
權條件變動時，可能面臨被動局面，長期而言亦限制公司在技術差異化上的
發展空間。

### Page 5

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

5 群益投顧 
規模成長不必然帶動利潤率同步提升：在固定利潤百分比的框架下，營收規
模的擴大主要反映的是晶圓與供應鏈成本的等比例放大，而非公司自身附
加價值的提升。換言之，世芯-KY 的獲利成長高度依賴出貨量的持續擴張，
而非利潤率的結構性改善，此一特性使公司的盈利模式更接近於量能驅動
型 (Volume-driven)，而非價值驅動型(Value-driven)。  
整體而言，世芯-KY 等 IC 設計服務公司的商業模式恰好能針對不同客戶的
能力缺口提供精準介入。無論客戶需求是後端實體設計落地、部分實體設計
協作、I/O Chiplet 的 IP 整合，乃至純量產委外，世芯均具備對應的服務能
量。此種高度彈性的模組化接案模式，使公司得以滲透不同成熟度的客戶群
體，有效降低拿單門檻。 
總而言之，CSP 晶片自研能力的持續演進是評估世芯-KY 長期投資價值不
可迴避的核心風險。從演進路徑來看，CSP 通常由依賴完整 ASIC 服務起步，
逐步過渡至主導架構設計但委外實體落地，最終邁向全面設計自主。世芯-
KY 目前所享有的設計服務需求，本質上是 CSP 處於此過渡階段所產生的結
構性紅利，一旦 CSP 設計能力全面成熟，世芯-KY 的角色將面臨從「策略
性設計夥伴」退化為「可替代供應鏈中介」的根本性風險，商業模式的可持
續性將受到挑戰。短期而言，N3 乃至 N2 先進製程實體設計的高度複雜性，
以及多代合作累積的工程默契，仍構成一定的切換成本緩衝。然而隨著 EDA
工具智能化持續壓低設計門檻，此一護城河的侵蝕速度是要警惕。群益認
為，世芯-KY 能否在當前紅利窗口期內成功深化 IP 能力、拓展先進封裝技
術佈局並實現客戶多元化，將是決定其長期商業模式能否升級躍遷的關鍵
觀察指標。 
N3 專案為 2026 年和 2027 年主要的營收貢獻： 
世芯-KY 目前有超過 60 項 FinFET 專案和 20 個以上 CoWoS 案子，平均每
年 20~30 new tape-out，且獲得多個 N3 專案，也有 N2 專案進行中。其中最
重要的專案是為北美 CSP 開發的 N3 AI 加速器(Trainium 3)。該專案在 4Q25
啟動，2Q26 末已進入量產。N5 完整的專案營收貢獻約 35 億美元，N3 的營
收貢獻會高很多，除主要客戶專案之外，世芯-KY 還贏得來自北美客戶的多
個 N3 專案，包括網路客戶以及北美和歐洲的主要網路設備廠商。 
現行 N3 晶片已符合各項測試標準且良率維持在合理水準，目前正全速出
貨，為 2026 年最大營收貢獻專案，亦將維持為 2027 年最大營收來源。管理
層預期當前世代專案將量產約 2 年時間，隨後由次世代接替。該專案預期於
4Q26 營收呈現小幅季增，並於 2027 年呈現大幅增長。世芯-KY 過去因營運
資金有限而在該專案初期未深化溝通，但後續成功自競爭對手處贏回此專
案，目前主要營運與產能限制已獲得解決，已做好承接更大出貨規模之準
備。 
取得美系新客戶訂單，預期於 2027 年躍升為世芯-KY 第二大客戶： 
世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽訂重大合約並展開執行，該專案整
體生命週期累計營收規模約 10 億美元，量產營收預計自 2Q27 開始貢獻，
後續逐季增加。已正式生效並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季
維持季增趨勢，該專案確定應用於 AI 領域，推估採用台積電 N3 先進製程，
專案資產由雙方共同擁有。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對象，非
中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出規模，市場推測可能

### Page 6

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

6 群益投顧 
為潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之北美雲端巨頭 Meta 或 Microsoft；
世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切入合作，有效緩解客戶端工
程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全
新商業模式之開端，具備良好獲利能力且長期可持續，該新客戶自 2Q27 起
之量產放量將推動其躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。 
2027 年 N3 案子貢獻持續，及美系新客戶加入，預估 2026 年和 2027 年稅後
EPS 分別為 113.66 元和 193.02 元： 
世芯-KY 2026 年 ~2028 年營收將會再大幅成長，因為 1)北美大客戶新世代
產品 N3 量產和下一代設計合作，公司預計其關鍵客戶在 2028 年前將有強
勁且可持續的營收貢獻；營收從 2Q26 開始顯著躍升。2)美系大型巨頭新客
戶的 10 億美元，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季季增。3)車用業務
ADAS 方面，預計產品將成為從 2026 年開始的主要營收貢獻者之一。下一
代產品的開發已在進行中，確保營收連續性並為業務帶來強勁動能，直到
2028 年。 
受惠於 N3 AI加速器量產出貨小幅季增，疊加次世代 N2 專案展開流片(Tape-
out)與大型設計案啟動，4Q26 營收預估將達到 8.5~9 億美元區間，改寫單季
歷史新高。由於量產營收比重仍高達 80%以上，4Q26 毛利率預估將維持在
18~19.5%區間，公司預期 2026 年全年毛利率將達 20%以上。預估 2026 年
稅後 EPS 113.66 元。 
邁入 2027 年，1Q27 營收預估小幅拉回，自 2Q27 起，隨著規模達 10 億美
元的美國新大客戶量產營收正式啟動，營運將重啟逐季強勁增長動能，單季
營收將突破 10 億美元大關。3Q27 至 4Q27 在現有 N3 專案擴大出貨以及次
世代 N2 加速器於 4Q27 展開小量產(Low volume production) 挹注下，預估
2027 年稅後 EPS 193.02 元。 
2028 年次世代 N2 加速器進入量產： 
展望 2028 年，世芯-KY 大客戶的次世代 N2 加速器進入大規模量產階段，
由於 N2 專案轉向全環繞閘極(GAA)與 Chiplet 先進架構，世芯-KY 表示將
負責全晶片實體設計與整合，晶片價值量與專案規模將顯著超越 N3 世代。
因此管理層評估 2028 年營收具備再次翻倍之潛力，但群益先以保守情境預
估。 
在營運擴張過程中，世芯-KY 面臨兩大核心營運限制，分別為營運資金
(Working Capital)承載力以及高階研發人力瓶頸。在資金層面，管理層明確
指出 2028 年營收成長的瓶頸已非市場需求，而是資產負債表的承載能力。
由於雲端服務商(CSP)先進製程晶圓與先進封裝代工代採購金額極為龐大，
支撐 40 億美元以上營收需要巨額營運資金墊付晶圓廠貨款，公司資產負債
表結構將直接決定能承接的量產規模上限。在人力層面，單一先進製程大型
專案往往需要高達 150 位工程師投入，在 N3 出貨、N2 實作與新客戶專案
同步推進下，研發資源短缺成為主要限制，迫使公司採取高度篩選專案之策
略，僅承接具備實質商業營收之大型案件。為突破研發人力天花板，世芯-
KY 目前正積極對外擴編，預計 2026 年底員工人數將突破千人規模，並在
前端實作與驗證導入專屬內部 AI 訓練模型以大幅節省中段研發人力，但在
最終實體設計階段仍高度仰賴頂尖工程人力，高階人才擴充速度將是公司
長期承接新專案的關鍵決定因素。

10 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

1 
4540 全球傳動(TBI MOTION)

報告日期：2026/11/19 研 究 員：洪 國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 財報受惠產品組合優化，呆帳回沖，業外匯兌利益挹注，
單季 EPS 0.79 元，優於先前預期 0.44 元。 
 蘇州新廠將於 1Q~2Q27 正式投產，將有助舒緩台灣樹林廠產
能吃緊情況。 
 桃園大溪廠己於 4Q26 正式處分，預計 1Q27 交割完成，處分
利益 1.1 億元，貢獻全球傳動 EPS 0.95 元。 
一. 公告 3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.79 元 
在客戶訂單回溫，全球傳動 3Q26 營收金額 9.46 億元，
YoY+40.27%，QoQ+13.96%，營業利益 0.81 億元，較 3Q25 由
虧轉盈，QoQ+63.74%，稅後盈餘 0.92 億 元，較 3Q25 由虧轉盈，
QoQ+80.7%，稅後 EPS 0.79 元。1Q~3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.04 元，
優於去年同期-1.08 元。 
二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 0.56 元 
全球 10/2026 營收金額 2.88 億元，YoY+11.5%，MoM-11.14%。
全球傳動 4Q26 將致力優化產品組合為目標，預估全球傳動 4Q26
營收金額 9.36 億元，YoY+21.5%，QoQ-1.05%，預估營業利益
0.59 億元，較 4Q25 由虧轉盈，QoQ-27.6%，預估稅後盈餘 0.64
億元，YoY+38.2%，QoQ-30.1%，預估稅後 EPS 0.56 元。 
三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 1.60 元 
全球傳動 2026 年傳動元件產品如行星滾柱螺桿與微型螺桿持續
切入機器人，半導體與自動化設備領域，產能利用率回溫，帶動
由虧轉盈。預估全球傳動 2026 年營收金額 35 億元，YoY+36.8%，
預估稅後盈餘 1.84 億元，由虧轉盈，預估稅後 EPS 1.60 元。 
四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 3.30 元 
全球傳動 2027 年在中國大陸持續大力發展機器人領域，將有助全
球行星滾柱螺桿與微型螺桿產品切入，另外大溪廠處分利益 1Q27
認列，有助全球傳動 2027 年營運表現。預估全球傳動 2027 年營
收金額 40 億元，YoY+14.3% ，預估稅後盈餘 3.80 億元，
YoY+105.97%，預估稅後 EPS 3.30 元。 
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 66 元 
觀察機器人執行機構件包括旋轉執行器，線性執行器，末端執行
器，全球傳動己小量成功打入線性執行器與靈巧手廠商供應鏈，
將有助評價提升，故投資建議買進，目標價 66 元。(28X 2027 年
扣除大溪廠土地後稅後 EPS=20X 2027 年稅後 EPS ) 
投資評等：買進

2

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
全球傳動科技(TBI MOTION)成立於 2010 年，資 本 額 11.52 億元，
並於 2018 年掛牌上市，全球傳動為台灣傳動元件專業製造廠，TBI 
MOTION 掌握關鍵核心技術，專注創新研發與設計，擁有多項產
品設計專利並且通過 ISO9001 認證，經銷營業據點分佈全球，廣
獲各國客戶的肯定。TBI MOTION 產品線齊全、MIT 台灣製造生
產，主要產品：滾珠螺桿、線性滑軌、滾珠花鍵、旋轉式滾珠螺
桿/花鍵、單軸機器人、直線軸承、聯軸器、螺桿支撐座..等。 產
品的應用範圍非常廣泛，實際應用於自動化產業、半導設備、產
業機械、工具機產業、綠能產業與機器人相關產業。全球傳動營
收地區方面，中國大陸 74~82%、台灣地區 7~10%、亞洲地區
6~10%、歐洲地區 3~5%、美洲地區 1%。

(單軸機器人)。

圖二、全球傳動產品

預估 2027 年滾珠螺桿營收金額 21.00 億元，YoY+12.9%

滾珠螺桿主要功用在於藉由鋼珠於螺桿與螺帽之間進行運動，由
於滾珠螺桿在頻繁返復運轉時，鋼珠間會產生摩擦熱度導致出現
氧化或脫碳現象而縮短使用壽命，全球傳動滾珠螺桿以轉造級螺
桿為主，所提供標準規格為 6mm~80mm，亦可提供研磨級螺桿規
格為 4mm~160mm，螺帽部分則以循環方式區分內循環、外循環、
端蓋式循環。滾珠螺桿 2025 年營收金額 14.73 億元，
YoY+0.00%，在中國大陸製造業景氣低迷，導致客戶訂單量維持
在低檔水準。

滾珠螺桿 1Q~3Q26 營收金額 13.59 億元，YoY+33.27%。預 期 2026
年，全球傳動己切入人形機器人的關鍵零組件，包括線型執行器
與靈巧手。觀察一台人型機器人需要用到 14 個線型執行器，一個
線型執行器需要一台行星滾柱螺桿；靈巧手方面，一台人型機器
人需要兩隻靈巧手，每隻靈巧手需要 15 支微型滾珠螺桿。上述都
己分別打入台灣與中國大陸機器人相關供應鏈，預期 2026 年開始
小量出貨。另外半導體則切入後段封裝設備廠，預期機器人及半
導體可望為營運成長動能。宏遠預估 2026 年滾珠螺桿相關產品營
收金額 18.60 億元，YoY+2633%。

預期 2027 年，在滾珠螺桿在自動化設備與機器人市場規模持續擴
大帶動下，另外微小型研磨螺桿己打入 CPO 設備模組供應鏈，將
有助滾珠螺桿出貨持續成長，宏遠預估 2027 年滾珠螺桿相關產品
營收金額 21.00 億元，YoY+12.9%。

預估 2027 年線性滑軌營收金額 16.35 億元，YoY+14.3%

線性滑軌屬滾動導引裝置，主要藉由鋼珠於滑塊與滑軌之間作無
限滾動循環，以維持負載平台順沿滑軌進行高精度線性運動，其
中技術層次要求須將線性滑軌摩擦係數降至傳統滑動導引的
1/50，以及滑塊與滑軌間末制單元設計須能同時承受各方向負
荷。全球傳動標準型線性滑軌尺吋涵蓋 15mm~65mm，另可提供
7mm~15mm 之微小型滑軌。線性滑軌 2025 年營收金額 9.41 億
元，YoY+35.2%。

預估 2027 年滾珠花鍵與其他營收金額 2.65 億元，YoY+26.2%

滾珠花鍵特色相較於滾珠花鍵可大負荷容量、旋轉方向零間隙、
高度靈敏性、高剛性與裝配簡單等。其他產品主要以單軸機器人
為主，單軸機器人採用線軌與螺桿的優點，將螺帽與滑塊設計成
一體式機構，並搭配高剛性的 U 型軌最佳化斷面，可達到最佳的
空間節省及大幅度減少組裝的時間，確保其高剛性、高精度要求。
滾珠花鍵與其他產品 2025 年營收金額 1.46 億元，YoY+16.1%。

在自動化智慧工廠趨勢明確，全球將持續開發新產品，加上產能
擴充帶動，將有助滾珠花鍵與單軸機器人恢復成長動能，預估滾
珠花鍵與其他 2026 年營收金額 2.1 億元，YoY+44.03%。

### Page 6

元

球行星滾柱螺桿與微型螺桿產品切入，加上蘇州新廠將於 1Q27
正式投產，另外大溪廠處分利益 1Q27 認列，有助全球傳動 2027
年營運表現。預估全球傳動 2027 年營收金額 40.00 億元，
YoY+14.28%，預估營業利益 3.23 億元，YoY+118.85%，預估稅
後盈餘 3.80 億元，YoY+105.97%，預估稅後 EPS 3.30 元。

## Document 237: [000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.11.02  
잠시 쉬어갑니다

3Q26 Review 
기아는 3Q26P 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.4% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회했다. 영업이익 증
감 요인은 환율 효과(+5,290억원), 물량 효과(+4,420억원), 믹스 효과(-4,650억원), 
인센티브/가격 효과(-7,270억원), 재료비 영향(-3,680억원), 기타(+4,130억원)이다. 
판매 성장에도 높아진 수익성 부담 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.9% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.1만대
(+8.8%)를 기록했다. 미국 HEV와 서유럽 EV 판매가 각각 118%, 163% 증가하며 
글로벌 점유율은 4.0%까지 확대됐다. 다만, 판매 성장에도 원화 강세와 원자재 가격 
상승, EV 비중 확대에 따른 믹스 부담으로 수익성은 전분기 대비 하락했다. 유럽 내 
EV 판매가 늘어나면서 믹스가 악화되었으며, 전쟁으로 인한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 
재료비 부담은 전년 대비 약 3,700억 증가했다.  
연간 판매목표 335만대는 유지했으나, 4분기 수익성의 급격한 반등은 어려울 전망이
다. 향후 미국 텔루라이드·스포티지 HEV 공급 확대와 유럽 EV 판매 증가에 따른 고정
비 흡수가 수익성 회복의 핵심이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 155,000원으로 하향 
기아에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하고, 실적 추정치 하향을 반영해 목표주가를 
155,000원으로 하향한다. 재료비 부담, 환율 효과 축소, 예상보다 큰 믹스 악화로 단
기 이익 눈높이 조정은 불가피하다. 다만 산업수요 둔화에도 주요 시장에서 점유율을 
확대되고 있다는 점은 긍정적이다. 현재 배당 수익률은 7% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높
은 배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

## Document 299: Anthropic上市前触发自身安全警报：AI进展速度超预期

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic上市前触发自身安全警报：AI进展速度超预期.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 在 IPO ⽂件中披露， AI 研发进展速度已触发公司额外安全机制。评估机构 METR
发现， Anthropic 过去⼀年的技术进展相当于此前两年， AI 参与⾃⾝研发正加速这⼀进程。
递归⾃我改进带来的失控⻛险，也引发 AI 安全领域担忧。
摘要：
Anthropic 即将登陆华尔街之际，却在上市⽂件中披露了⼀项安全警报：公司 AI 研发进展速度已
达到⾃⾝设定的安全阈值，必须启动额外安全控制措施。
11 ⽉ 19 ⽇，据英国《⾦融时报》报道，Anthropic 在更新后的 IPO 申报⽂件中承认，其技术进展
速度已触发 2023 年制定的 “ 负责任扩展政策 ” （ Responsible Scaling Policy ）中的额外安全机
制。这⼀披露恰逢公司将 IPO 发⾏价定为每股 83 美元，⾼于此前指引区间，估值约达 1.5 万亿美
元之际。
触发安全机制的依据，来⾃ AI 评估机构 METR 本⽉发布的⼀份报告。该机构在获得 Anthropic 系
统、⼈员及未发布模型的⼴泛访问权限后发现， Anthropic 过去⼀年的 AI 能⼒进展相当于此前约

两年的历史进展。研究⼈员借助 AI ⼯具加速⾃⾝研发，正成为推动这⼀趋势的重要因素。
AI 进展速度翻倍，触发内部安全机制
METR 是⼀家专注于评估前沿 AI 能⼒及⻛险的研究机构。其最新报告显⽰， Anthropic 过去 12 个
⽉实现了约两年的历史技术进展，能⼒提升速度较此前⼀年⼤约翻倍。
这⼀速度触及 Anthropic ⾃⾝设定的安全阈值。根据公司 2023 年制定的政策，当 AI 发展速度达到
将过去两年的技术进展压缩⾄⼀年内实现的程度时，公司必须执⾏更严格的安全监督措施。
Anthropic 表⽰，此次判断基于对未来模型能⼒的预期及整体进展速度，并不意味着 AI 系统已经
能够完全⾃主改进。新增措施将涉及安全保障、治理架构以及模型开发过程中的内部审查。
Anthropic ⾼管 Sam McCandlish 回应称， METR 报告中的许多结论与⾃⼰的判断⼀致。他承认，
AI 正在加速公司的研究⼯作，但这⼀影响仍难以精确量化。
AI 参与⾃⾝研发，递归改进⻛险升温
技术进展提速背后，是 AI 开始越来越多地参与下⼀代 AI 系统的研发。这⼀过程被称为 “ 递归⾃我

改进 ” （ RSI ），即 AI 系统利⽤⾃⾝能⼒协助开发更强⼤的后续模型。
Anthropic 在招股说明书中将⼀个模型协助构建下⼀个模型的 “ 闭环 ” 研发能⼒，列为⾃⾝相较竞争
对⼿的重要优势，并称其已显著提升算⼒使⽤效率。
但这⼀能⼒也带来新的安全⻛险。Apollo Research ⾸席执⾏官 Marius Hobbhahn 表⽰，研究
⼈员此前对递归⾃我改进的实现时间存在很⼤分歧，如今这⼀时间窗⼝正迅速缩短，⽽⾏业的
安全准备远未到位。
AI 安全机构 “AI 未来项⽬ ” （ The AI Futures Project ）认为， METR 报告揭⽰的加速趋势出乎意
料，并预测超⼈类 AI 研究员很可能在 2027 年某个时间点出现。该机构认为，暂停或实质性放缓
前沿 AI 开发已⼗分迫切。

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
We reaffirm our expectation that the RBA Monetary Policy
Board will again increase the cash rate at the November
meeting. Today’s inflation data and Senate testimony are
the final pieces of information before the RBA Monetary
Policy Board (MPB) meets next week. Recent data and

communication have consistently pointed to the staff
recommending an increase in the cash rate. We expect a
majority of the external members to agree, though some
members might dissent.
Inflation is above target, and above what the RBA’s August
forecasts implied. Some upside risks identified in August have
partially materialised, particularly energy prices. The RBA is
especially worried that above-target inflation will become
embedded in people’s expectations and pricing decisions. It

will therefore deem that further policy tightening is needed.
The RBA has made it clear that it is down-weighting the recent
easing in the labour market, even though this was more than it
forecast. In today’s opening statement to the Senate hearing,
the Governor said, “Despite the unemployment rate recently
rising a little more than we had expected, the labour market
seems a little tighter than we had previously judged. The
evidence for this is elevated underlying inflationary pressures.

That is, when we exclude some of the large volatile price
movements such as fuel we see inflationary pressure in areas
that reflect domestic demand for goods and services.” In the
Senate, the Governor also said that they were not worried
about the current pace of increase in unemployment.
Of course, this takes no account of the lagged relationship
between the labour market and inflation. The argument is that
if that measure of inflation last quarter was too high, it must

therefore be that the labour market is too tight. Nor does it
allow sufficiently for non-labour cost shocks, which business
surveys indicate are the real issue. But whether this judgement
by the RBA turns out to be right or not will not be revealed
for some time, certainly not soon enough to change its mind
before next week. And while some MPB members might have
a more optimistic view about labour supply trends than the
RBA staff, they are likely to be a minority.

The RBA is also relatively sanguine about the current
downturn in the housing market. The Governor and other
RBA officials are at pains to highlight that they do not target
housing prices; nor should they. The RBA considers that most,
though not all, of the current downswing is an expected effect
of tighter monetary policy.
RBA to hike in November, as inflation remains too high
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
The question then turns to the future beyond November. While

we still expect a hawkish tone in the RBA’s communication
until inflation has clearly turned down, further hikes beyond
next week are not currently our base case. Clearly, though, the
risks of another hike – most likely in February – have increased.
Our current forecast for Q4 inflation would seem to give them
cover for such a move. We note, however, that home-building
costs in the CPI have undershot expectations in the past couple
of months. This might imply a different view of the extent of

domestic demand pressures than the RBA currently believes.
However, a lot can happen in three months, as we have just
seen. The conflict in the Middle East could escalate further,
lifting energy prices beyond our current base case. It could
also go the other way, though, if the US and Iran manage to
strike a post-midterm-election deal. And as their counterparts
in New Zealand highlighted today, one risk is that the global
economy weakens suddenly. In the post-meeting statement,

the RBNZ Monetary Policy Committee said, “the world
economy could tip abruptly into a downturn causing weaker
inflation over the medium term.” This risk was something the
RBNZ MPC thought could crystallise in the next six weeks.
Whether on this timetable or something more drawn out, this
possibility does not appear to be on the RBA’s radar. Nor is it
our base case, though we have previously worried that the AI
financing boom could hit a wall quite suddenly.

“We reaffirm our expectation that the RBA
Monetary Policy Board will again increase the
cash rate at the November meeting"

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related
risks through 2027; although, in the absence of another shock,
we expect them to remain on hold.
The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view

on the breadth of growth, the 3.4% annualised gain for GDP
benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household
consumption, 12.7% growth in non-residential investment
as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment
was effectively offset by net exports’ drag given most of
the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is
imported. This is not to say that US firms won’t benefit from

the investment once complete, but simply that much of the
initial return goes to the foreign businesses who provide the
necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks
remain for the consumer, the increase in nominal spending
seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis,
consumption flat in the month following a 0.5% gain in
August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the
outlook is expected to see household consumption growth

average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE
inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr medium-term target as
the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline
and core measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy
rates unchanged in October. Of note from the meeting
communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks

for inflation, but also downside risks for growth. "While the
full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying
inflation has remained broadly stable so far and wages do not
show a material response". "Financing conditions have [also]
tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth".
In the press conference, President Lagarde noted that,
between the October and December meetings, a significant
amount of new economic information will be released,

allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.
Based on the October meeting communications, we continue
to expect a rate hike at the December meeting but believe this
will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the
spectrum of risks Europe faces is broad and skewed towards
inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that
she will step down as ECB President in February ahead of the

scheduled conclusion of her term next October.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI.
Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in the month, largely
owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in
domestic and international holiday travel & accommodation.
The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than
anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).
While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation –
housing in particular looks to be cooling faster than expected

as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are
still showing persistence. Highlighting this is market services
ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator
of price pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this
metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail points
to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for
another chunky read in Q4.
With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts,

and some of the energy risks flagged then materialising, we
continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp
rate hike to 4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate
hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market
expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding instead to wait for
further information before taking the next step with policy. The
decision was made 4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A

25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening in 2027
(Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems
probable; however, each decision will depend on fuel prices,
inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October
meeting. Based on prior comments by key members, the
decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook

and Cleveland Fed President Hammack’s dissents in favour
of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at
a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in September). Chair
Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic
growth outside of tech-related investment (evinced by Q3
GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in
the statement. However, inflation “remains elevated” and the

FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable
return of annual inflation to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target.
This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will
help the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests
another 25bp hike is likely in December. Thereafter, the FOMC
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

### Page 4

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
A lesson to be learned
The RBNZ delivered the biggest shock to markets outside of
a crisis period in over 20 years this week by leaving the OCR
unchanged. Markets had priced around a 98% probability of
a 25bp increase, and all major domestic analysts concurred.

After the CPI data the week earlier, we belatedly shifted our
call to a hike despite plenty of good arguments in favour of
the RBNZ sticking to its plan to pause. With the benefit of
hindsight, too much weight was put on market pricing and
short-term inflation drivers and not enough on the medium
term. That’s an important lesson to be learned.
The highlight of an action-packed week of central banking news
was the unexpected decision of the RBNZ to leave the OCR at

2.75%, despite near unanimous analyst expectations and market
pricing in favour of a 25bp hike. We had shifted sides from a
hold to a hike just a week earlier, in the wake of a CPI outcome
that was weaker than RBNZ expectations on the surface, but
with some evidence of firm core inflation undertones.
While we clearly cannot claim to have gotten this call right,
we always thought that the outcome was not the “done deal”
that markets had priced in. For example, in our preview of the

meeting we noted:
“We still think a more measured tightening approach is
appropriate from here. Raising the OCR at every meeting
until something ‘breaks’ could be a risky strategy if downside
growth concerns crystalise.”
And we saw a vote on the decision as very possible:
“A vote seems very possible as differences in view will likely
remain among the MPC about how much the medium-term
inflation outlook has really shifted in recent weeks.”
The 4:3 vote that was ultimately required to bring the MPC

to a decision is a testament to just how finely balanced the
decision was. We had thought that the hike was perhaps
around a 60-70% probability whereas the Record of Meeting
implies something more like a 50:50 call.
In the final analysis, the RBNZ has merely followed through on
the pause it signalled for this meeting back in the September
Monetary Policy Statement. All MPC members continue to see
a case for a higher OCR in time, but there are different views

around the timing of those increases. Governor Breman noted
that further interest rate increases remain likely in coming
meetings but are conditional on the whether the outlook
evolves in line with expectations. Indeed, the Governor noted
in later comments to press that a December hike was a “high”
probability and that the outlook remains for OCR “hikes” as the
base case. This suggests that the RBNZ’s view on the terminal
OCR hasn’t really shifted down from the 3.25% level presented

back in September. And it may be reasonable to speculate,
subject to data evolving as the RBNZ expects, that a modest
upgrade in those forward OCR forecasts could be in the offing.
So how did we all get this so wrong? This outcome was a real
shock given the historical record shows that over the history of
the OCR the market has without exception correctly predicted
a 25bp hike when the implied probability built into markets
was as high as it was. Clearly some recalibration of the model

is required. We can identify two key areas where lessons
should be learned: too much weight on market pricing; and not
enough on drivers of medium-term inflation.
Market pricing did not adequately reflect the
MPC’s desire to shift strategy.
Firstly, and most importantly, we put too much weight on
market pricing and too little on the RBNZ’s clearly stated
strategy of preferring a pause at the October meeting to
assess the impact of interest rate increases already done. In our

Preview we incorrectly surmised that silence equated to assent
and that market pricing of a hike would make an OCR hike the
less disruptive option – especially given the sense that a hike
to 3% was still likely in December as implied by the RBNZ’s
September forecasts. From a macroeconomic perspective,
raising rates six weeks early is of little consequence.
This logic, as sound as it was, was wrong, as market pricing did
not reflect a large enough change in the medium-term inflation

outlook to justify a change in RBNZ strategy. Markets at least
partially held the view, based on a long history of revealed
RBNZ preference, that the RBNZ keeps moving the OCR in the
same direction until confronted with compelling evidence that
a halt to the cycle is in prospect. That operating approach may
be under review now.
It’s not uncommon for other central banks to take breaks
while amid an interest rate cycle to gather information on
the impact of work already done before moving forward.

This week’s pause by the FOMC in the face of a fierce set of
monthly inflation readings in the last month is an example.
Pausing does not need to imply stopping or reversing. Indeed,
taking a more measured approach could prove to be solid
risk management – as we identified in our MPR preview note.
Risk management concerns probably should have greater
prominence in this cycle given we are seeing a measure of
Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist NZ

### Page 5

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
supply side cost shocks that might not be persistent. The
Statement of Record contains a wealth of material related to
these sorts of uncertainties and concerns. Markets were not
listening to these concerns, however.
An insufficient focus on drivers of medium-term

inflation
Markets were not listening to the alarm bells because there
was a heavy focus on factors that may be more relevant to the
short-term inflation outlook than medium-term inflation. Top
of mind were the rise in energy prices and fall in the exchange
rate. Both factors look set to significantly boost the short-
term inflation outlook – for example we currently forecast
December quarter annual inflation of 4.3%, with upside risks
from there if fuel prices do not fall over the balance of the

quarter. However, our analysis of the data surprises suggested
no significant shift to the implied RBNZ OCR track one year
ahead. Upward pressure on the future OCR projection from
the lower exchange rate and stronger GDP was balanced
by higher long term interest rates and the lower Q3 CPI
outcome. Higher energy prices were not seen as a key factor
as they don’t impact medium term inflation unless inflation
expectations are dislodged (no evidence was found of that

since September) or if the increase in energy prices is viewed
as persistent (futures markets continue to imply lower forward
energy prices, although MPC hawks clearly see risks of
persistent inflation coming from supply shocks).
The RBNZ’s communications at the MPR and in its aftermath
are directing markets to pay more attention to medium-term
inflation drivers in the future. Hence the references to the
strength of demand and inflation expectations. The risk to

the RBNZ’s inflation forecasts are to the upside, and hence
we might reasonably expect some upgrade of the OCR profile
when we see new forecasts in December. But it’s not a done
deal by any means as data and events between now and then
will dictate what stance the RBNZ takes. This week’s business
and consumer confidence reports do not seem to present many
smoking guns of imminent second-round inflation pressures,
even though business and consumer sentiment seems to be

taking the higher oil price in its stride.
Is core inflation high enough yet to shift the
strategy?
Another interesting point made by the Governor was that the
markets (and analysts) misinterpreted how the September
quarter CPI was read by the RBNZ, which evidently took
greater comfort from the outcome. Governor Breman has noted
she wished the RBNZ has protocols in place to communicate
with markets the RBNZ’s view on that CPI data given that it
seems to have been a factor that figured importantly in at least

some MPC member’s thinking. Headline inflation was lower
than the RBNZ expected and importantly the core inflation
measures, that we had thought might have been of concern to
the MPC, do not yet seem to have been at a level sufficient to
shift the RBNZ from its pause-then-hike strategy. We remain
sceptical that a 3.25% OCR will prove sufficient to bring core
inflation down significantly. But core inflation appears to need
to lift for the MPC to become more agitated. It likely will lift,

but we won’t get more data on that until January 2027.
December hike likely but not a done deal
We expect a further 25bp hike at this year’s final meeting on 9
December and then 25bp increases in the February and March
2027 meetings, followed by a slower pace of increases at the
June and September Monetary Policy Statement meetings.
Our forecast of a peak OCR of 4% from September 2027 has
remained unchanged throughout, as has our assessment of a
3.75% neutral OCR.

That said, in the current uncertain environment, it goes without
saying that the evolution of monetary policy will depend on
how both global events and key data evolve. Therefore, as the
RBNZ noted itself, the exact timing of the tightening profile
is highly uncertain and even the tightening we forecast at the
December meeting should not be regarded as a done deal.
Looking ahead, the ‘hardest’ data to come will be the Q3
labour market surveys where we anticipate a small fall in

the unemployment rate to 5.5% but still moderate wage
pressures. The December quarter RBNZ inflation expectations
surveys will be of significant interest (17, 20 and 24
November) for MPC members looking for persistent inflation
impacts. The usual run of monthly activity and selected prices
indicators will be useful in narrowing down Q4 growth and
inflation momentum. And finally, the MPC’s focus on external
risks will imply keeping an eye on global fixed income, equity

and credit markets for signs of imminent calamity.
Finally, November 7 is the general election. A close race is
envisaged that could impact on the RBNZ’s mandate. We
will be offering client briefings on Monday 9 November and
Tuesday 10 November to outline the immediate implications
of the election outcome for markets and the economy. Contact
your relationship manager for details.

We reaffirm our expectation that the RBA Monetary Policy
Board (MPB) will decide to raise the cash rate by 25bps to
4.85% at the November meeting.

Inflation is above target, and above what the RBA’s August
forecasts implied. Some upside risks identified in August have
partially materialised, particularly energy prices. The RBA is
especially worried that above-target inflation will become
embedded in people’s expectations and pricing decisions.
Recent data and communication have consistently pointed to
the RBA staff recommending an increase in the cash rate. We
expect a majority of the external board members to agree,

though some members might dissent.
AUS: Oct Cotality Home Value Index (%mth)
Nov 2, Last: –1.1, Westpac f/c: –1.3
The Cotality home value index – covering all of Australia – fell
1.1% in Sep with downward revisions shaving a further 0.4%
off prices. The correction has now seen prices decline 5.2%
from their March peak, annual growth stalling flat. Cotality
reported 97% of capital city suburbs experienced falls over the
three months to September.
The October update is set to be another weak one with an

added hit coming from the RBA’s interest rate rise in late
September. The daily measure (covering the five major capital
cities rather than all of Australia) is tracking a 1.5% decline vs
1.3% in September. The detail points to slightly bigger falls
in Sydney, Brisbane and Perth with Melbourne continuing
to record more moderate declines. Note that seasonality is
usually a slight positive in the October month.
AUS: Sep Dwelling Approvals (%mth)
Nov 2, Last: –6.1, Westpac f/c: –7.0

Market f/c: 2.0, Range: –7.0 to 5.0
Dwelling approvals fell 6.1%mth in August, with the annual
pace easing slightly to 10.3%yr. Weakness centred on unit
approvals with private detached house approvals actually
posting a solid rebound from a July month fall, touching a new
cycle high (up 18.4%yr).
We expect the September update to show a much clearer
weakening. New construction is coming under pressure
from multiple fronts: interest rate rises; a sharp downturn in

established markets; and rising costs – with a great deal of
uncertainty around each of these. New home sales have fell
sharply through the September quarter and we expect to
see detached house approvals show something similar. Unit
approvals are coming off a weak read and should be more
stable. Total approvals are expected to print 7% lower.

### Page 7

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.7
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Sep Goods Trade Balance ($bn)
Nov 5, Last: 0.5, Westpac f/c: 2.0
Market f/c: 1.0, Range: –1.0 to 2.5
Australia’s goods trade surplus narrowed to $0.5bn in August.
Recently, the trade balance has been oscillating between
small deficits and surpluses with significant volatility in the

underlying trade mix.
Swings in non-monetary gold, fuel and cars have all played
a large role, but the most eye-catching result from Aug was
the 79% surge in automatic data processing equipment (ADP)
imports, which captures the investment upswing related to
data centres.
For September, we expect an increase in the surplus as the
ADP import surge unwinds, though we are likely in store for
ongoing monthly volatility in trade for some time.
NZ: Sep Building Consents (%mth)

Nov 3, Last: +5.6%, Westpac f/c: Flat
Annual residential building consent numbers are currently at
a three year high, and we expect that they will remain firm in
the September update. However, monthly consent numbers
are showing signs of peaking, with falls in three of the last four
months. We’re watching to see if that trend has continued,
particularly given the ongoing softness in the housing
market.  In the non-residential space, we’ve seen softness

in the number of retail and office developments, but greater
resilience in the industrial and storage segment.
AUS: Sep Household Spending Indicator (%mth)
Nov 4, Last: flat, Westpac f/c: –0.5
Market f/c: 0.2, Range: –0.5 to 0.8
The household spending indicator was unchanged in August,
annual growth easing to 6.8%yr. The detail showed a modest
rotation away from discretionary categories towards non-
discretionary.
Our Westpac-DataX Card Tracker showed a decent rise in

the September month although new vehicle sales (which are
not covered by the tracker but are in scope for the spending
indicator) look to have posted a decline. Overall we expect
the indicator to show a 0.5% decline in the September month.
Note that the update with also include Q3 estimates of real
spending – we expect this to show a 0.6%qtr gain with prices
up 1.2%qtr and nominal spend up 1.8%qtr.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: Q3 Labour Market Surveys (%)
Nov 4, Unemployment Rate, Last: 5.6, Westpac f/c: 5.5
LCI Private Sector, Last: 0.7, Westpac f/c: 0.5
We expect the unemployment rate to edge down to 5.5%
for the September quarter. Job numbers have been gaining
momentum in recent months, and now look to be enough to

outstrip population growth. However, the existing slack in the
labour market is likely to be keeping wage growth subdued,
despite cost-of-living pressures on workers. Our forecasts are
a little more positive overall than what the RBNZ assumed in
its September MPS forecasts.
US: Oct Employment Report
Nov 6, Payrolls, Last: 29k, Market f/c: 55k, WBC f/c: 75k
Nov 6, U/E rate, Last: 4.2%, Market f/c: 4.2%, WBC f/c: 4.3%
Nonfarm payrolls have shown considerable volatility of late,

July’s -10k followed by a 133k gain in August then a 29k
increase in September. However, the 3-month average of 51k
is likely a good guide on underlying momentum. In October, we
look for a 75k gain, although we expect some of that increase
to be offset by a modest reduction to the prior two months,
keeping the 3-month average around 50k.
While the participation rate has lifted in recent months, it
remains well below the average of 2025. Month-to-month, a

change either way is not guaranteed, but it seems likely that
the participation rate will trend higher in coming months. We
therefore expect the unemployment rate to edge up.

## Document 318: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-13)

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years.1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.
